print("[SHIPPUDEN] boot (PC & Mobile Universal fix)...")
pcall(function()
    if not game:IsLoaded() then
        local t0 = tick()
        while not game:IsLoaded() and (tick() - t0) < 6 do
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
    end
end)

local Players         = game:GetService("Players")
local TweenService    = game:GetService("TweenService")
local Lighting        = game:GetService("Lighting")
local CoreGui         = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local LP              = Players.LocalPlayer
if not LP then
    local t0 = tick()
    while not Players.LocalPlayer and (tick() - t0) < 5 do
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
    LP = Players.LocalPlayer
end

local function getSafeGuiParent()
    if gethui then
        local ok, h = pcall(gethui)
        if ok and h then return h end
    end
    if syn and syn.protect_gui then
        local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui")
        local ok = pcall(function()
            local t = Instance.new("Folder")
            syn.protect_gui(t)
            t.Parent = cg
            t:Destroy()
        end)
        if ok then return cg end
    end
    local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    local ok = pcall(function()
        local t = Instance.new("Folder")
        t.Parent = cg
        t:Destroy()
    end)
    if ok then return cg end
    return LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5) or LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
end

-- [ZYNN] Ping Bypass removed

-- ============================================================
-- zynn INTRO: a abertura dura 10 segundos desde que a tela aparece.
-- Skip Intro encerra a abertura e para a musica imediatamente.
-- A faixa enviada e baixada uma vez e reproduzida como arquivo local pelo executor.
-- Reproduzir MP3 por URL diretamente em SoundId nao funciona no Roblox.
-- ============================================================
local INTRO_DURATION = 10
local INTRO = {
    enabled = false,
    music = 1,
    matchEnabled = false,
    matchMusic = 1,
    animeUrl = "",
    animeAsset = "",
    OPTIONS = {},
    stopPreview = nil,
    stopPlayback = nil,
    getSongName = nil,
    preview = nil,
    play = nil,
    playMatch = nil,
    stopMatch = nil,
    getMatchSongName = nil,
}

(function()
    local SoundService = game:GetService("SoundService")

    local INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS = {
        {name="RyzenxHub Intro", url="https://project--86964ab4-117d-44b0-9478-4dc13565dcc9-dev.lovable.app/__l5e/assets-v1/62bda52a-420e-4b45-98e6-c3c2f5798ec7/RyzenxHub_IntroSong_10.mp3", file="RyzenxHub_IntroSong_10.mp3", startTime=0},
        {name="Song 2", url="https://files.catbox.moe/2a7jyx.mp3", file="ZynnIntroSong_2.mp3"},
        {name="Song 3", url="https://files.catbox.moe/rcgr9f.mp3", file="ZynnIntroSong_3.mp3"},
        {name="Song 5", url="https://files.catbox.moe/6eigoh.mp3", file="ZynnIntroSong_5.mp3"},
        {name="Song 7", url="https://files.catbox.moe/iyw1cb.mp3", file="ZynnIntroSong_7.mp3"},
        {name="Song 8", assetId="rbxassetid://76650356472656", startTime=3, volume=1},
        {name="Song 9", url="https://files.catbox.moe/mzvrir.mp3", file="ZynnIntroSong_1.mp3"},
        {name="Song 11", url="https://files.catbox.moe/2a7jyx.mp3", file="ZynnRandomSong_11.mp3"},
        {name="Song 12", url="https://files.catbox.moe/rcgr9f.mp3", file="ZynnRandomSong_12.mp3"},
    }

    local introPreviewSound = nil
    local introPlaybackSound = nil
    local introPreviewToken = 0
    local introPlaybackToken = 0
    local introSongCache = {}
    local introSongDownloading = {}

    local function stopIntroPreview()
        introPreviewToken = introPreviewToken + 1
        if introPreviewSound then
            pcall(function() introPreviewSound:Stop() end)
            pcall(function() introPreviewSound:Destroy() end)
            introPreviewSound = nil
        end
    end

    local function stopIntroPlayback()
        introPlaybackToken = introPlaybackToken + 1
        if introPlaybackSound then
            pcall(function() introPlaybackSound:Stop() end)
            pcall(function() introPlaybackSound:Destroy() end)
            introPlaybackSound = nil
        end
    end

    local function getIntroSongName()
        local opt = INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS[INTRO.music]
        return opt and opt.name or "No Songs Added"
    end

    local function _safeNotify(msg)
        print("[SHIPPUDEN] " .. tostring(msg))
    end

    local function _httpGetBody(url)
        if not url or url == "" then return nil end
        local body = nil
        local function tryReq(fn)
            if body and #body > 1000 then return end
            pcall(function()
                local res = fn()
                if type(res) == "string" and #res > 1000 then
                    body = res
                elseif type(res) == "table" then
                    local b = res.Body or res.body or res.Data or res.data
                    if type(b) == "string" and #b > 1000 then body = b end
                end
            end)
        end
        tryReq(function()
            if request then return request({Url = url, Method = "GET"}) end
        end)
        tryReq(function()
            if http_request then return http_request({Url = url, Method = "GET"}) end
        end)
        tryReq(function()
            if syn and syn.request then return syn.request({Url = url, Method = "GET"}) end
        end)
        tryReq(function()
            if fluxus and fluxus.request then return fluxus.request({Url = url, Method = "GET"}) end
        end)
        tryReq(function()
            return game:HttpGet(url)
        end)
        tryReq(function()
            return game:HttpGetAsync(url)
        end)
        return body
    end

    local function cacheIntroSong(option, allowDownload)
        if option and option.assetId then return option.assetId end
        if not option or not option.url or option.url == "" then return nil end
        if not getcustomasset then return nil end
        local fileName = option.file or ("ZynnIntroSong_" .. tostring(option.name or "song") .. ".mp3")
        local function loadExisting()
            if introSongCache[fileName] then return introSongCache[fileName] end
            local hasFile = false
            pcall(function() hasFile = isfile and isfile(fileName) end)
            if hasFile then
                local ok = pcall(function() introSongCache[fileName] = getcustomasset(fileName) end)
                if ok and introSongCache[fileName] then return introSongCache[fileName] end
            end
            return nil
        end
        local cached = loadExisting()
        if cached then return cached end
        if allowDownload == false then return nil end
        if introSongDownloading[fileName] then
            local waitStart = tick()
            while introSongDownloading[fileName] and tick() - waitStart < 20 do task.wait(0.05) end
            cached = loadExisting()
            if cached then return cached end
        end
        introSongDownloading[fileName] = true
        local urls = { option.url }
        if option.url:find("litter.catbox.moe", 1, true) then
            table.insert(urls, (option.url:gsub("litter%.catbox%.moe", "files.catbox.moe")))
        elseif option.url:find("files.catbox.moe", 1, true) then
            table.insert(urls, (option.url:gsub("files%.catbox%.moe", "litter.catbox.moe")))
        end
        for attempt = 1, 3 do
            for _, u in ipairs(urls) do
                local data = _httpGetBody(u)
                if data and #data > 1000 and writefile then
                    local wok = pcall(function()
                        writefile(fileName, data)
                        introSongCache[fileName] = getcustomasset(fileName)
                    end)
                    if wok and introSongCache[fileName] then
                        introSongDownloading[fileName] = nil
                        return introSongCache[fileName]
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.25 * attempt)
        end
        introSongDownloading[fileName] = nil
        return loadExisting()
    end

    local function preloadIntroSongs()
        task.spawn(function()
            cacheIntroSong(INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS[INTRO.music], true)
            for _, option in ipairs(INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS) do
                if option ~= INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS[INTRO.music] then
                    cacheIntroSong(option, true)
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function makeIntroSoundFromId(soundId, name, parent)
        if not soundId then return nil end
        local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
        sound.Name = name or "ZynnIntroMusic"
        sound.Volume = 0.65
        sound.Looped = false
        sound.SoundId = soundId
        sound.Parent = parent or SoundService
        return sound
    end

    local function createIntroSound(option, fileName, parent, allowDownload)
        if not option then return nil end
        local soundId = cacheIntroSong(option, allowDownload)
        if not soundId then return nil end
        local sound = makeIntroSoundFromId(soundId, fileName, parent)
        if sound and option.volume then sound.Volume = option.volume end
        return sound
    end

    local function previewIntroMusic(index)
        stopIntroPreview()
        stopIntroPlayback()
        if not INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS[index] then _safeNotify("ADD SONG LINKS"); return end
        local token = introPreviewToken
        task.spawn(function()
            local option = INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS[index]
            local sound = createIntroSound(option, "ZynnIntroPreview_" .. tostring(token), SoundService, true)
            if token ~= introPreviewToken then if sound then sound:Destroy() end; return end
            introPreviewSound = sound
            if not sound then _safeNotify("SONG LOADING..."); return end
            local loadStart = tick()
            while sound and not sound.IsLoaded and tick() - loadStart < 15 do task.wait(0.05) end
            if sound and sound.Volume < 0.1 then sound.Volume = 0.65 end
            sound.TimePosition = tonumber(option.startTime) or 0
            pcall(function() sound:Play() end)
            task.delay(15, function() if token == introPreviewToken then stopIntroPreview() end end)
        end)
    end

    local function playIntroMusic()
        stopIntroPreview()
        stopIntroPlayback()
        if not INTRO.enabled then return end
        local option = INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS[INTRO.music]
        if not option then return end
        local token = introPlaybackToken
        task.spawn(function()
            local sound = createIntroSound(option, "ZynnIntroMusic_" .. tostring(token), SoundService, true)
            if token ~= introPlaybackToken then if sound then pcall(function() sound:Destroy() end) end; return end
            introPlaybackSound = sound
            if not sound then return end
            local loadStart = tick()
            while sound and not sound.IsLoaded and tick() - loadStart < 18 do task.wait(0.05) end
            -- O download pode terminar depois do Skip ou dos 10 segundos.
            -- Nesse caso, nunca iniciar a musica atrasada.
            if token ~= introPlaybackToken then
                pcall(function() sound:Destroy() end)
                return
            end
            if sound and sound.Volume < 0.1 then sound.Volume = 0.65 end
            sound.TimePosition = tonumber(option.startTime) or 0
            pcall(function() sound:Play() end)
            if INTRO._matchSound then pcall(function() INTRO._matchSound:Pause() end) end
        end)
    end

    local function playIntroAnimation()
        -- Intro removida
        return
    end

    INTRO.OPTIONS = INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS
    INTRO.stopPreview = stopIntroPreview
    INTRO.stopPlayback = stopIntroPlayback
    INTRO.getSongName = getIntroSongName
    INTRO.preview = previewIntroMusic
    INTRO.play = playIntroAnimation
    INTRO._matchToken = 0

    INTRO.stopMatch = function()
        INTRO._matchToken = (INTRO._matchToken or 0) + 1
        if INTRO._matchSound then
            pcall(function() INTRO._matchSound:Stop() end)
            pcall(function() INTRO._matchSound:Destroy() end)
            INTRO._matchSound = nil
        end
        pcall(function()
            if _G.ZynnMatchUI and _G.ZynnMatchUI.refresh then _G.ZynnMatchUI.refresh() end
        end)
    end

    INTRO.MATCH_OPTIONS = {
        {name="Yo Voy Voy", url="https://litter.catbox.moe/xkew3553w380fzfb.mp3", file="ZynnIntroSong_10.mp3"},
        {name="Tryhard", assetId="rbxassetid://77142753938657", volume=0.7},
        {name="Tryhard BR 1", assetId="rbxassetid://135750430892149", volume=0.7},
        {name="STRINGS FUNK", url="https://litter.catbox.moe/rvi4fpjia6bgvzi3.mp3", file="MiseryMatchSong_4.mp3"},
        {name="CUIDA DO PET", url="https://litter.catbox.moe/dw8fcc21n2cjtr20.mp3", file="MiseryMatchSong_5.mp3"},
        {name="Ultra Maligna", url="https://litter.catbox.moe/e3bu4b0q7hxx1i1k.mp3", file="MiseryMatchSong_6.mp3"},
        {name="Tryhard BR 2", url="https://litter.catbox.moe/0fq7y2kjpxn7wc6b.mp3", file="MiseryMatchSong_7.mp3"},
        {name="Random Drop 1", url="https://files.catbox.moe/iyw1cb.mp3", file="ZynnRandomMatch_1.mp3"},
        {name="Random Drop 2", url="https://litter.catbox.moe/m493detpu1asdmin.mp3", file="ZynnRandomMatch_2.mp3", startTime=51},
        {name="Random Drop 3", assetId="rbxassetid://76650356472656", startTime=3, volume=0.7},
        {name="Random Drop 4", url="https://files.catbox.moe/mzvrir.mp3", file="ZynnRandomMatch_4.mp3"},
        {name="Random Drop 5", url="https://litter.catbox.moe/xkew3553w380fzfb.mp3", file="ZynnRandomMatch_5.mp3"},
    }

    INTRO.getMatchSongName = function()
        local opt = INTRO.MATCH_OPTIONS[INTRO.matchMusic]
        return opt and opt.name or "No Song"
    end

    INTRO.playMatch = function()
        INTRO.stopMatch()
        if not INTRO.matchEnabled then return end
        local option = INTRO.MATCH_OPTIONS[INTRO.matchMusic]
        if not option then return end
        local token = INTRO._matchToken
        task.spawn(function()
            local sound = createIntroSound(option, "MiseryMatchMusic_" .. tostring(token), SoundService, true)
            if token ~= INTRO._matchToken then
                if sound then pcall(function() sound:Destroy() end) end
                return
            end
            INTRO._matchSound = sound
            if not sound then return end
            sound.Looped = false
            sound.Volume = option.volume or 0.65
            local loadStart = tick()
            while sound and not sound.IsLoaded and tick() - loadStart < 15 do task.wait(0.05) end
            if token ~= INTRO._matchToken then return end
            sound.TimePosition = 0
            pcall(function() sound:Play() end)
            pcall(function()
                sound.Ended:Connect(function()
                    if token == INTRO._matchToken and INTRO.matchEnabled and INTRO._matchSound == sound then
                        sound.TimePosition = 0
                        pcall(function() sound:Play() end)
                    end
                end)
            end)
            pcall(function()
                if _G.ZynnMatchUI and _G.ZynnMatchUI.refresh then _G.ZynnMatchUI.refresh() end
            end)
        end)
    end

    preloadIntroSongs()
end)()

-- ============================================================
-- SHIPPUDEN - BLACK THEME + TAB SYSTEM
-- ============================================================

local RunService      = game:GetService("RunService")
local UIS             = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local HttpService     = game:GetService("HttpService")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local _isfile   = isfile   or (syn and syn.isfile)   or function() return false end
local _readfile = readfile  or (syn and syn.readfile)  or function() return nil  end
local _writefile= writefile or (syn and syn.writefile) or function() end
local getconnections = getconnections or get_signal_cons or getconnects or (syn and syn.get_signal_cons)

-- Backgrounds portados do XZZ DUELS
_G.TITLE_STYLE_IDS = {"109374981553919", "109374981553919"}
local MISERY_BACKGROUND_IDS = {
    "138986505892159", -- unica foto mantida (segunda miniatura escolhida)
}

-- ============================================================
-- CUSTOM TOOLS + CUSTOM SOUNDS (port Ambitious: Bat / Medusa skins)
-- ============================================================
_G.ShippudenToolAssets = {
    DiamondSword = {
        cat = "Bat", label = "Diamond Sword",
        mesh = "rbxassetid://8827558932", tex = "rbxassetid://8827558969",
        scale = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2),
        c0 = CFrame.new(-0.05, -0.1, -0.12) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), math.rad(180), math.rad(300)),
        view = 4.9,
    },
    Katana = {
        cat = "Bat", label = "Katana",
        mesh = "rbxassetid://13528902482", tex = "rbxassetid://13528902373",
        scale = Vector3.new(1.4, 1.4, 1.4),
        c0 = CFrame.new(0, 0.6, 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(270), math.rad(180), math.rad(180)),
        view = 6.6,
    },
    Skull = {
        cat = "Medusa", label = "Skull",
        mesh = "rbxassetid://2050312704", tex = "rbxassetid://2050313393",
        scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1),
        c0 = CFrame.new(0, 0.65, -0.4) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(330), 0, 0),
        view = 4.2,
    },
    GoldenDesertEagle = {
        cat = "Medusa", label = "Golden Desert Eagle",
        mesh = "rbxassetid://430251413", tex = "rbxassetid://435840335",
        scale = Vector3.new(0.01, 0.01, 0.01),
        c0 = CFrame.new(0, 0, -0.8) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(330), math.rad(180), 0),
        view = 3.0,
    },
}
_G.ShippudenToolSkins = _G.ShippudenToolSkins or { Bat = "DiamondSword", Medusa = "GoldenDesertEagle" }
_G.ShippudenCustomToolsEnabled = false
_G.ShippudenCustomSoundsEnabled = false

do
    local ASSETS = _G.ShippudenToolAssets
    local SOUNDS = {
        Bat = { id = "rbxassetid://5713085119", skip = 0.2 },
        Medusa = { id = "rbxassetid://3102797479", doublePlay = true, delay = 0.5 },
    }
    local CT_ITEMS = {
        { key = "Bat", match = function(n) return n:lower():find("bat") ~= nil or n:lower():find("slap") ~= nil end },
        { key = "Medusa", match = function(n) return n:lower():find("medusa") ~= nil end },
    }
    local function matchItem(inst)
        if not inst or not inst:IsA("Tool") then return nil end
        for _, item in ipairs(CT_ITEMS) do
            if item.match(inst.Name) then return item end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function skinFor(cat)
        local key = _G.ShippudenToolSkins[cat]
        if key and ASSETS[key] and ASSETS[key].cat == cat then return key end
        return (cat == "Bat") and "DiamondSword" or "GoldenDesertEagle"
    end

    local toolsOn, soundsOn = false, false
    local tracked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local soundTracked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local watchers = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

    local function buildReplica(handle, modeKey)
        local existing = handle:FindFirstChild("LocalReplica")
        if existing then existing:Destroy() end
        local data = ASSETS[modeKey]
        if not data then return end
        local m = Instance.new("Model")
        m.Name = "LocalReplica"
        local p = Instance.new("Part")
        p.Name = "MainPart"
        p.CanCollide = false
        p.CanQuery = false
        p.CanTouch = false
        p.Massless = true
        p.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
        p.Transparency = 0
        p.Anchored = false
        p.Parent = m
        local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
        mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
        mesh.MeshId = data.mesh
        mesh.TextureId = data.tex
        mesh.Scale = data.scale
        mesh.Parent = p
        local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
        weld.Part0 = handle
        weld.Part1 = p
        weld.C0 = data.c0 or CFrame.new()
        weld.Parent = p
        m.Parent = handle
    end

    local function hideOriginals(entry, tool)
        for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:FindFirstAncestor("LocalReplica") then continue end
            if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Name ~= "Handle" then
                if entry.hidden[d] == nil then entry.hidden[d] = d.Transparency end
                d.Transparency = 1
            elseif d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture") then
                if entry.hidden[d] == nil then entry.hidden[d] = d.Transparency end
                d.Transparency = 1
            elseif d:IsA("SpecialMesh") and not d:FindFirstAncestor("LocalReplica") then
                if entry.hidden[d] == nil then entry.hidden[d] = true end
                d.Scale = Vector3.zero
            end
        end
        local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        if handle and handle:IsA("BasePart") then
            if entry.hidden[handle] == nil then entry.hidden[handle] = handle.Transparency end
            handle.Transparency = 1
        end
    end

    local function restoreOriginals(entry)
        for obj, val in pairs(entry.hidden or {}) do
            pcall(function()
                if typeof(val) == "number" and (obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture")) then
                    obj.Transparency = val
                end
            end)
        end
        entry.hidden = {}
    end

    local function setupTool(tool)
        if tracked[tool] then return end
        local item = matchItem(tool)
        if not item then return end
        local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        if not handle then return end
        local entry = { hidden = {}, cat = item.key, conns = {} }
        tracked[tool] = entry
        if toolsOn then
            hideOriginals(entry, tool)
            buildReplica(handle, skinFor(item.key))
        end
        table.insert(entry.conns, tool.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
            if not parent then
                for _, c in ipairs(entry.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
                tracked[tool] = nil
            end
        end))
    end

    local function teardownTool(tool)
        local entry = tracked[tool]
        if not entry then return end
        for _, c in ipairs(entry.conns or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        if handle then
            local rep = handle:FindFirstChild("LocalReplica")
            if rep then pcall(function() rep:Destroy() end) end
        end
        restoreOriginals(entry)
        tracked[tool] = nil
    end

    local function setupSound(tool)
        if soundTracked[tool] then return end
        local item = matchItem(tool)
        if not item then return end
        local data = SOUNDS[item.key]
        if not data then return end
        local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        if not handle then return end
        local old = handle:FindFirstChild("CustomSound")
        if old then old:Destroy() end
        local cs = Instance.new("Sound")
        cs.Name = "CustomSound"
        cs.SoundId = data.id
        cs.Volume = 1
        cs.Parent = handle
        local entry = { sound = cs, data = data, conns = {}, muted = {} }
        soundTracked[tool] = entry
        local function muteOrig(snd)
            if not snd:IsA("Sound") or snd.Name == "CustomSound" then return end
            if entry.muted[snd] then return end
            entry.muted[snd] = true
            table.insert(entry.conns, snd.Played:Connect(function()
                pcall(function() snd:Stop() end)
                pcall(function()
                    cs:Stop()
                    cs.TimePosition = data.skip or 0
                    cs:Play()
                end)
                if data.doublePlay then
                    task.delay(data.delay or 0.5, function()
                        if cs and cs.Parent then
                            pcall(function() cs.TimePosition = data.skip or 0; cs:Play() end)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end))
        end
        for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do muteOrig(d) end
        table.insert(entry.conns, tool.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
            task.defer(function() muteOrig(d) end)
        end))
    end

    local function teardownSound(tool)
        local entry = soundTracked[tool]
        if not entry then return end
        for _, c in ipairs(entry.conns or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        if entry.sound then pcall(function() entry.sound:Destroy() end) end
        soundTracked[tool] = nil
    end

    local function scanAll()
        local function scan(container)
            if not container then return end
            for _, ch in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                if ch:IsA("Tool") and matchItem(ch) then
                    if toolsOn then setupTool(ch) end
                    if soundsOn then setupSound(ch) end
                end
            end
        end
        scan(LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
        scan(LP.Character)
    end

    local function watch(container)
        if not container or watchers[container] then return end
        watchers[container] = container.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
            if not ch:IsA("Tool") then return end
            if not matchItem(ch) then return end
            task.defer(function()
                if toolsOn then setupTool(ch) end
                if soundsOn then setupSound(ch) end
            end)
        end)
    end

    function _G.ShippudenSetCustomTools(on)
        toolsOn = on and true or false
        _G.ShippudenCustomToolsEnabled = toolsOn
        if toolsOn then
            watch(LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
            watch(LP.Character)
            scanAll()
        else
            for tool in pairs(tracked) do teardownTool(tool) end
        end
    end

    function _G.ShippudenSetCustomSounds(on)
        soundsOn = on and true or false
        _G.ShippudenCustomSoundsEnabled = soundsOn
        if soundsOn then
            watch(LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
            watch(LP.Character)
            scanAll()
        else
            for tool in pairs(soundTracked) do teardownSound(tool) end
        end
    end

    function _G.ShippudenSetToolSkin(cat, key)
        if not ASSETS[key] or ASSETS[key].cat ~= cat then return end
        _G.ShippudenToolSkins[cat] = key
        if State then
            if cat == "Bat" then State.customToolSkinBat = key end
            if cat == "Medusa" then State.customToolSkinMedusa = key end
        end
        for tool, entry in pairs(tracked) do
            if entry.cat == cat and tool.Parent then
                local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                if handle then pcall(buildReplica, handle, key) end
            end
        end
        task.spawn(saveConfig)
    end

    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        task.wait(0.4)
        if toolsOn or soundsOn then
            watch(char)
            scanAll()
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        watch(LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
        LP.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
            if c:IsA("Backpack") then watch(c) end
        end)
    end)
end



-- TAUNT x3 portado do XZZ DUELS (globais para evitar o limite de locals do Luau)

function _G.ZynnApplyTitleStyle(idx)
    local ids = _G.TITLE_STYLE_IDS
    if not ids or #ids == 0 then
        ids = {"125831394220618"}
        _G.TITLE_STYLE_IDS = ids
    end
    idx = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(idx) or 1), 1, #ids)
    if State then State.currentTitleStyle = idx end
    task.spawn(function() pcall(saveConfig) end)
    local id = tostring(ids[idx] or "125831394220618")
    local asset = "rbxassetid://" .. id
    local function applyTo(img)
        if not img then return end
        pcall(function()
            img.Image = asset
            img.ImageTransparency = 0
            img.Visible = true
            if img:IsA("ImageLabel") or img:IsA("ImageButton") then
                img.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
            end
        end)
    end
    pcall(function()
        -- VENOM HUB: hide the old title artwork (it contains the previous Shippuden branding).
        if _G.ZynnLogoImg then
            _G.ZynnLogoImg.Image = ""
            _G.ZynnLogoImg.Visible = false
        end
        applyTo(_G.ZynnMiniBtn)
        -- Do not scan generic Logo/Title images here: that can restore the old
        -- Shippuden artwork over the VENOM HUB title when Title Style changes.
        -- Only update the dedicated mini button; keep the old logo hidden.
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            local CP = game:GetService("ContentProvider")
            local tmp = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            tmp.Image = asset
            CP:PreloadAsync({tmp})
            tmp:Destroy()
            -- Keep the old title artwork hidden; the VENOM HUB text label is the visible title.
            if _G.ZynnLogoImg then
                _G.ZynnLogoImg.Image = ""
                _G.ZynnLogoImg.Visible = false
            end
            applyTo(_G.ZynnMiniBtn)
        end)
    end)
end

_G.ZynnTauntMessage = "MISERY ON TOP LOL"
_G.ZynnTauntRepeatCount = 3
_G.ZynnTauntMessageDelay = 0.35
_G.ZynnTauntQueue = 0
_G.ZynnTauntWorkerRunning = false

function _G.ZynnSendTauntChatMessage()
    local sentWithTextChat = false
    pcall(function()
        local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
        if TextChatService.ChatVersion ~= Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService then return end
        local channels = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("TextChannels")
        if not channels then return end
        local channel = channels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
        if not channel then
            for _, candidate in ipairs(channels:GetChildren()) do
                if candidate:IsA("TextChannel") then channel = candidate; break end
            end
        end
        if channel then
            channel:SendAsync(_G.ZynnTauntMessage)
            sentWithTextChat = true
        end
    end)
    if sentWithTextChat then return true end

    local legacySent = false
    pcall(function()
        local events = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
        local sayMessage = events and events:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest")
        if sayMessage then
            sayMessage:FireServer(_G.ZynnTauntMessage, "All")
            legacySent = true
        end
    end)
    return legacySent
end

function _G.ZynnRunTaunt()
    _G.ZynnTauntQueue = (_G.ZynnTauntQueue or 0) + 1
    if _G.ZynnTauntWorkerRunning then return end
    _G.ZynnTauntWorkerRunning = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while (_G.ZynnTauntQueue or 0) > 0 do
            _G.ZynnTauntQueue = _G.ZynnTauntQueue - 1
            for index = 1, (_G.ZynnTauntRepeatCount or 3) do
                _G.ZynnSendTauntChatMessage()
                if index < (_G.ZynnTauntRepeatCount or 3) then
                    task.wait(_G.ZynnTauntMessageDelay or 0.35)
                end
            end
            if (_G.ZynnTauntQueue or 0) > 0 then
                task.wait(_G.ZynnTauntMessageDelay or 0.35)
            end
        end
        _G.ZynnTauntWorkerRunning = false
    end)
end

function _G.ZynnPositionToConfig(position)
    if typeof(position) ~= "UDim2" then return nil end
    return {
        X = {Scale = position.X.Scale, Offset = position.X.Offset},
        Y = {Scale = position.Y.Scale, Offset = position.Y.Offset},
    }
end

function _G.ZynnConfigToPosition(data, fallback)
    if type(data) ~= "table" then return fallback end
    local x = type(data.X) == "table" and data.X or {}
    local y = type(data.Y) == "table" and data.Y or {}
    return UDim2.new(
        tonumber(x.Scale) or fallback.X.Scale,
        tonumber(x.Offset) or fallback.X.Offset,
        tonumber(y.Scale) or fallback.Y.Scale,
        tonumber(y.Offset) or fallback.Y.Offset
    )
end

function _G.ZynnCleanupOldESPVisuals()
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local character = player.Character
        if character then
            for _, existing in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
                if existing:IsA("BillboardGui") and (existing.Name == "MiseryESP_Avatar" or existing.Name == "EnemySpeedGui") then
                    pcall(function() existing:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    for _, existing in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
        if existing:IsA("Highlight") and existing.Name == "NoxyESP_HL" then
            pcall(function() existing:Destroy() end)
        end
    end
end

local antiLagDescConn = nil
local antiLagActive = false
local antiLagDefBrightness, antiLagDefFog, antiLagDefDiffuse, antiLagDefSpecular

local function _applyAntiLagObj(obj)
    pcall(function()
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            obj.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic; obj.Reflectance = 0; obj.CastShadow = false
        elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then
            obj.Transparency = 1
        elseif obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or obj:IsA("Trail") or obj:IsA("Beam")
        or obj:IsA("Fire") or obj:IsA("Smoke") or obj:IsA("Sparkles") then
            -- Skin smoke that was enabled in the original asset must survive Anti Lag.
            if not obj:GetAttribute("ZynnPreserveSkinSmoke") then
                obj.Enabled = false
            end
        elseif obj:IsA("AnimationController") or obj:IsA("Animator") then
            for _,t in ipairs(obj:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do pcall(function() t:Stop(0) end) end
        end
    end)
end

local function enableAntiLag()
    antiLagActive = true
    antiLagDefBrightness = antiLagDefBrightness or Lighting.Brightness
    antiLagDefFog        = antiLagDefFog        or Lighting.FogEnd
    antiLagDefDiffuse    = antiLagDefDiffuse    or Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale
    antiLagDefSpecular   = antiLagDefSpecular   or Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale
    Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
    Lighting.FogEnd = 1e10
    Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
    Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
    for _,e in pairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
        pcall(function()
            if e:IsA("BlurEffect") or e:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or e:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect")
            or e:IsA("BloomEffect") or e:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then e.Enabled = false end
        end)
    end
    for _,obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do _applyAntiLagObj(obj) end
    if antiLagDescConn then antiLagDescConn:Disconnect() end
    antiLagDescConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if antiLagActive then _applyAntiLagObj(obj) end
    end)
end

local function disableAntiLag()
    antiLagActive = false
    if antiLagDescConn then antiLagDescConn:Disconnect(); antiLagDescConn = nil end
    pcall(function()
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
        if antiLagDefBrightness then Lighting.Brightness = antiLagDefBrightness end
        if antiLagDefFog        then Lighting.FogEnd = antiLagDefFog end
        if antiLagDefDiffuse    then Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = antiLagDefDiffuse end
        if antiLagDefSpecular   then Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = antiLagDefSpecular end
        for _,e in pairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            pcall(function()
                if e:IsA("BlurEffect") or e:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or e:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect")
                or e:IsA("BloomEffect") or e:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then e.Enabled = true end
            end)
        end
    end)
end
